Objective function for L-BFGS:
```
def lbfgs_objective(a, mode, scale, K, lambda_reg, n_fft=None):
   a = np.clip(a,0,1)^? but minimize may pass out of bounds? L-BFGS-B respects bounds but initial. It can evaluate within.
   a = np.maximum(a,0)
   S=a.sum()
   if S < 1e-12: return inf, grad...
   c=linear_convolve(a)  # raw not normalized
   L = LSE or topK (c)
   score = 2nL/S^2
   # h derivative wrt raw a of L (not normalized). Since c(a) with raw, derivative h=2∑p a_{m-i} (uses raw a values)
   grad_score = 2n*(h/S^2 - 2L/S^3)
   grad_total = grad_score + 2*lambda_reg*(S-1)*np.ones(n)
   return score, grad_total
```

```
Gradient FFT for dense p:
```
def derivative_from_weights(a,p,threshold=None):
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1
   # direct if nonzero count small
   nz=np.nonzero(p > p_tol)[0]
   if len(nz) <= direct_threshold:
      h=zeros
      for m in nz: w=p[m]; ...
      return h
   else:
      p_rev=p[::-1]
      M2=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
      A=fft.fft(a.astype(float), M2)
      P=fft.fft(p_rev.astype(float), M2)
      conv=fft.ifft(A*P).real[:n+L]
      # extract d length n: conv[L-n : L][::-1]
      d=conv[n-1:L][::-1]  # since L-n=n-1
      return 2*d
```

def spectral_project(a, target_L, M, strength=0.5, iters=20, deadline=None):
   best=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a)
   D=fft.fft(np.concatenate([target_L, zeros M-L]), M)
   for each:
       A=fft.fft(a,M); B=A*A
       # factor = exp(strength*0.5*(log(D)-log(B)))
       # But if target fixed and a changes, target B? Use D/B.

       logmag=np.clip(np.log(Dabs+eps)-np.log(Babs+eps), -5,5)
       phase=0.5*(np.angle(D)-np.angle(B))
       factor=np.exp(strength*(logmag+1j*phase))

       # symmetrize
       A2=symmetrize_fft(A2)
       an=fft.ifft(A2).real[:n]; clip; normalize
       if time...
       if score improved best...
       a=an
   return best
```

`lbfgs_objective` should be vectorized and fast. Let's write carefully.

```
def lbfgs_objective(a, mode, beta_frac, K, lambda_reg, direct_threshold):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    # clip small negatives
    if a.size == 0: return inf,zeros
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-8:
       n=a.size; return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    c = linear_convolve(a)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cmax): ...
    if mode == 0:
       # LSE
       if cmax <= 1e-300: ...
       scaled = beta_frac * (c - cmax) / cmax
       scaled = np.clip(scaled, -700.0, 0.0)
       e = np.exp(scaled)
       esum = float(e.sum())
       L = cmax + (cmax / beta_frac) * np.log(esum)
       p = e / esum
    else:
       K = int(K)
       Lc = len(c)
       if K <= 1:
          idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
          L = c[idx[0]]
       else:
          K = min(K, Lc)
          if K == Lc:
             idx = np.arange(Lc); L = float(c.mean())
          else:
             idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
             L = float(c[idx].mean())
       p = np.zeros(Lc, dtype=float)
       p[idx] = 1.0 / len(idx)
    # gradient h
    h = derivative_2weights(a,p,direct_threshold)  # returns 2∑p a
    S2=S*S; S3=S2*S
    grad = (2.0 * a.size) * (h / S2 - (2.0 * L) / S3)
    reg = lambda_reg * (S - 1.0)
    grad = grad + reg
    val = (2.0 * a.size) * L / S2 + 0.5 * lambda_reg * (S - 1.0)**2
    if not np.isfinite(val): return 1e100, np.zeros_like(a)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)
    return float(val), grad
```

def spectral_project_from_target(a, target_base, M, blend, max_iters, deadline):
   a=normalize(a); L=...
   best= a, sc
   D_base=fft.fft(np.concatenate(...)).
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time>deadline break
      progress=it/max_iters or 1
      lam = 0.1 + 0.7*progress
      A=fft.fft(a,M); B=A*A; c=ifft(B).real[:L]; c=np.maximum(c,0)
      if sc... update best with c max?
      target = (1-lam)*c + lam*target_base; normalize target sum S^2? Since c sum S^2. For normalized a S=1, sum c=1. target should sum1. normalize.
      D=fft.fft(extend target,M)  # heavy
      logmag=...
      factor=exp(0.5*blend*(logD-logB))
      A2=A*factor; sym; an=ifft.real; clip; normalize
      a=an
   return best

    nonimp=0
    while time<deadline:
       c=linear_convolve(a); cmax=...; score=2n*cmax
       if score<best: update
       idx topK; p; Lmean; b=derivative_2weights(a,p, threshold)/2
       raw = -eta * n * n * a * (b - Lmean)
       cweight = float(np.dot(a,raw)); raw -= cweight
       raw = np.clip(raw, -0.5, 0.5)
       an=a*np.exp(raw); S=an.sum(); if S>0 an=an/S
       sc_new=quick_score(an) # can reuse c? But c changed; compute quick. To save, compute score from an conv? quick.
       if sc_new < score: a=an; eta=min(eta*1.05,0.2); nonimp=0
       else: eta*=0.7; if eta<1e-4: break; nonimp+=1; if nonimp>50 break
    return best
```
But computing quick_score after update does another FFT. We could compute conv in loop and for new score use linear_convolve(an) again. That's okay.
Maybe use Lmean objective for step, but exact score for accept.
Eta scale: We used raw=-eta*n*n*a*(b-L). If b-L ~1/n, a~1/n, raw~eta*n? Wait n^2*a*(b-L)=n^2*(1/n^2)=1, good. eta start 0.02. Clip 0.3.
However after update, we normalize; if raw centered weighted sum zero, sum remains ~1. Good.
Direct b via derivative returns 2∑p a; divide by2. For topK p uniform. If mode weighted topK, p weights. We can use same function with p.

This multiplicative descent is essentially same as L-BFGS gradient but fixed step. It can run long. Use after L-BFGS for polish.

Potential risk: It may reduce variables to zero and concentration; track best.

Length change after optimization: We can resample best to neighboring lengths and try same optimizers. For example, if best length 16384, resample to 32768 and 8192, run short spectral/log. Since larger length may improve. At final, compare.
In propose, after main optimization, if time remains:
- resample best to pow2 lengths not tried (e.g. 16384,32768 if best smaller). For each, quick score; if within best+0.01, optimize briefly.
This captures length effect.

But final program complex. Need ensure no syntax/runtime errors.
```

```
`next_len`:
```
def next_len(n):
    n=int(max(1,n))
    try:
       return int(fft.next_fast_len(n))
    except Exception:
       return 1 << (int(n)-1).bit_length()
```
`(n-1).bit_length()` if n=1 ->0 bit? Use `m=1; while m<n: m<<=1`. Simpler fallback.

`linear_convolve(a)`:
```
n=len(a); L=2*n-1; M=next_len(L)
A=fft.rfft(a.astype(np.float64), M)
out=fft.irfft(A*A, M)[:L]
# remove tiny imaginary? irfft real
out[~np.isfinite(out)] = 0.0
# For nonnegative a, out should be >=0; clip tiny negatives only? If due error, out[out<0]=0
out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
return out
```
Clipping negative may slightly understate score; okay. But if numerical error causes meaningful negative? FFT with positive a should not have large negative. Use max.

`quick_score(a)`:
```

if S<1e-8: return np.inf
a=a/S
n=len(a)
c=linear_convolve(a)
sc=2.0*n*float(np.max(c)) # since S=1
return float(sc)
```
This normalizes. Good.

`resample_sequence(a,n)`:
```

```
`make_shape`:
No rng? We can use rng for random. But signature maybe `make_shape(n, shape_id, rng)`.
```
def make_shape(n, shape_id, rng=None):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    if shape_id==0: a=np.ones(n)
    elif 1: a=1+0.5*np.cos(np.pi*x) # edges high? x=0 1.5, x=1 0.5; nonneg
    elif 2: a=1+0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)
    elif 3: a=1+0.5*np.sin(np.pi*x) # center high 1.5
    elif 4: a=np.exp(-4.0*(x-0.5)**2)
    elif 5: a=np.sin(np.pi*x)**2
    elif 6: a=np.cos(0.5*np.pi*x)**2 # edge high
    elif 7: a=1+0.4*np.cos(4*np.pi*x)+0.2*np.sin(2*np.pi*x)
    elif 8:
       a=np.ones(n)
       if rng is not None:
          for _ in range(3):
             f=int(rng.integers(1,6))
             ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
             a = a * np.maximum(0, 1+0.3*rng.uniform(0.1,1.0)*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x+ph))
    else: a=np.ones(n)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-12: a=np.ones(n)/n
    else a=a/S
    return a
```
Need avoid variable in loop `amp = float(rng.uniform...)` not nested. Good.
```

```
`derivative_from_weights`:
Input a normalized or raw. p length L. work_limit. Returns h=2∑p a_{m-i}. For p all zeros? return zeros.
```
def derivative_from_weights(a,p,work_limit=3000000):
    n=int(a.size); L=2*n-1
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    if p.size != L: p = (p[:L] if p.size>L else np.pad(p,(0,L-p.size)))
    # tolerance
    tol = 1e-16 * (float(p.max()) if p.size else 0.0) + 1e-300
    nz = np.where(p > tol)[0]
    h=np.zeros(n,float)
    if nz.size == 0: return h
    if int(nz.size) * n <= int(work_limit):
       for jj in nz:
          m=int(jj); w=float(p[jj])
          if w == 0: continue
          i0 = m - (n - 1)
          if i0 < 0: i0=0
          i1 = m
          if i1 > n-1: i1=n-1
          if i1 >= i0:
             start = m - i0
             stop = m - i1 - 1
             # Need if stop < -1: stop = -1? but if m-i1=0 => stop=-1.
             if stop < -1: stop = -1
             # seg length?
             h[i0:i1+1] += 2.0 * w * a[start:stop:-1]
       return h
    else:
       p_rev = p[::-1]
       M2=next_len(3*n-2)
       A=fft.fft(a.astype(np.float64), M2)
       P=fft.fft(p_rev.astype(np.float64), M2)
       conv=fft.ifft(A*P).real[:n+L]
       d=conv[n-1:L][::-1].copy()
       return 2.0*d
```
Check slice `a[start:stop:-1]` with stop = -1. If start=0, `a[0:-1:-1]` okay? Let's be extra safe: Use if start==0 and stop== -1, segment length 1? Python: `np.arange(5)[0:-1:-1]` => array([0])? I think yes because stop -1 means index 4? Wait for negative stop, -1 corresponds last element excluded. For array length5, indices 0:-1:-1: start0, stop exclusive -1 (i.e. index4? no, -1 means n-1=4? With step -1, start>stop in indices? 0>4 false? Hmm Python normalizes stop -1 to 4. Then start 0 > stop 4 false -> empty? Let's recall: `lst[::-1]` full. `lst[0:-1:-1]` returns [lst[0]]? I know `lst[1:-1:-1]` returns []? Let's test mentally: Python slice with step -1: start index 0, stop index -1 normalized to 4. It iterates while index > stop. 0 > 4 false, so empty. But I have seen `a[::-1]` start None. `a[0:-1:-1]` maybe empty. To avoid uncertainty, use `=np.arange`? We can create segment by reversing a subarray simpler:
We want `a[start, start-1, ..., stop+1]` where stop exclusive. If stop == -1, stop+1=0. Use `seg = a[max(0, low):high+1][::-1]`? We need low = m - i1, high = m - i0. The indices in a are from low to high ascending; we need descending high to low. So `seg = a[low:high+1][::-1]`. This is safe and segment length = high-low+1 = i1-i0+1. Slice positive. Low≥0, high≤n-1. Yes! Use that.
```
low = m - i1
high = m - i0
h[i0:i1+1] += 2*w*a[low:high+1][::-1]
```
Example m=4,i0=0,i1=2: low=2,high=4 -> a[2:5][::-1]=4,3,2. Good. m=0,i0=0,i1=0 low0 high0 -> a[0:1][::-1]=[a0]. Good.
Use this.

For FFT extraction `conv[n-1:L]`: L=2n-1; slice end exclusive L, length L-(n-1)=n. Reverse. Good.

Potential negative values from FFT h; okay.

`lbfgs_objective` details. Need compute p for mode 2 with `beta_frac` maybe 0. If beta_frac<=0 use uniform.
```
def lbfgs_objective(a, mode, beta_frac, K, lambda_reg, work_limit):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
       return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    c=linear_convolve(a)
    Lc=c.size
    cmax=float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 1e-300:
       return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    if mode == 0:
       if beta_frac <=0: beta_frac=20.0
       scaled=(beta_frac*(c-cmax))/cmax
       scaled=np.clip(scaled,-700.0,0.0)
       e=np.exp(scaled)
       esum=float(e.sum())
       Lval=cmax + (cmax/beta_frac)*float(np.log(max(esum,1e-300)))
       p=e/esum
    elif mode == 2:
       K=int(max(1, K))
       if K <= 1:
          idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
          cidx=c[idx]
       else:
          K=min(K,Lc)
          if K >= Lc:
             idx=np.arange(Lc); cidx=c
          else:
             idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
             cidx=c[idx]
       if K <=1 or beta_frac <=0:
          w=np.full(idx.size,1.0/idx.size)
       else:
          cmaxidx=float(cidx.max())
          scaled=(beta_frac*(cidx-cmaxidx))/max(cmaxidx,1e-300)
          scaled=np.clip(scaled,-700.0,0.0)
          e=np.exp(scaled)
          esum=float(e.sum())
          if esum<=0: w=np.full(idx.size,1.0/idx.size)
          else: w=e/esum
       p=np.zeros(Lc); p[idx]=w
       Lval=float(np.dot(cidx,w))
    else:
       K=int(max(1, K))
       if K <= 1:
          m=int(np.argmax(c)); Lval=c[m]; p=np.zeros(Lc); p[m]=1.0
       else:
          K=min(K,Lc)
          if K>=Lc: idx=np.arange(Lc)
          else: idx=np.argpartition(c,-K)[-K:]
          Lval=float(c[idx].mean())
          p=np.zeros(Lc); p[idx]=1.0/idx.size
    if not np.isfinite(Lval) or Lval < 0:
       Lval=cmax
    h=derivative_from_weights(a,p,work_limit)
    S2=S*S; S3=S2*S
    if S2 <= 1e-30: return 1e100, zeros
    grad=(2.0*n)*(h/S2 - (2.0*Lval)/S3)
    reg=lambda_reg*(S-1.0)
    grad=grad+reg
    val=(2.0*n)*Lval/S2 + 0.5*lambda_reg*(S-1.0)*(S-1.0)
    # If p from topK, Lval is top mean. For mode0 LSE, Lval approx.
    if not np.isfinite(val): return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    grad=np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)
    return float(val), grad
```
For mode2 when K>=Lc, cmaxidx maybe cmax; w over all; L val smooth all. OK.
Potential issue: `argpartition(c, -K)` when K=Lc? We handle.
If c has all zeros? cmax small; handled.
```

```
`spectral_project` top-level:
```
def symmetrize_fft(A):
    M=A.size
    A=A.copy()
    A[0]=A[0].real
    if M % 2 ==0:
       half=M//2
       A[half]=A[half].real
       idx=np.arange(1,half)
    else:
       half=M//2
       idx=np.arange(1,half+1) # for odd, M=7 half3, pairs 1-6,2-5,3-4. idx 1,2,3; M-idx=6,5,4
    if idx.size>0:
       right = np.conj(A[M-idx])
       A[idx] = 0.5*(A[idx] + right)
       A[M-idx] = np.conj(A[idx])
    return A
```
For even M=8 half=4 idx 1..3; M-idx 7,6,5; Nyquist 4 real. Good.
For odd M=7 half=3 idx 1,2,3; M-idx 6,5,4. Good. But note A[half] for odd is not special; it gets paired with M-half =4? For M=7, half=3, pairs: 1<6,2<5,3<4. Yes idx includes 3 and M-idx includes 4. We didn't set A[3] real. Good.

`spectral_project(a0, target_mode, M, blend, deadline, target_base=None)`:
We can create target_base inside using make_target(L,mode). M passed maybe next_len(L) or 2*next_len(L). For interesting, use M=next_len(L) if L power? Actually if M=L and L not power, next_len(L) power maybe >L. Good. If target constant and M=power >L, nontrivial. For L=13923, next M=16384 >L nontrivial. Good. For L=32767, M=32768. Good. Use M=next_len(L). If M==L? If L power of two? L=2n-1 odd, can be power only L=1. So M>L. Good.
```
def spectral_project(a0, target_mode, blend, deadline, base_target=None):
    a=normalize(a0); n=len(a); L=2n-1; M=next_len(L)
    if base_target is None: base_target=make_target(L,target_mode)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=quick_score(a)
    D_base=fft.fft(np.concatenate([base_target, np.zeros(M-L)]), M) # but target changes with mix
    it=0; nonimp=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it +=1
        A=fft.fft(a,M)
        B=A*A
        c=fft.ifft(B).real[:L]
        c=np.maximum(c,0)
        sc=2*n*c.max()
        if sc < best_sc: best...
        progress=min(1.0, it/200.0)
        lam=0.15 + 0.75*progress
        target=(1-lam)*c + lam*base_target
        S=target.sum()
        if S>0: target=target/S
        else target=base_target
        D=fft.fft(np.concatenate([target, np.zeros(M-L)]), M)
        Babs=np.abs(B); Dabs=np.abs(D)
        tol=1e-300
        logmag=np.log(Dabs+tol)-np.log(Babs+tol)
        logmag=np.clip(logmag,-6,6)
        phase=0.5*(np.angle(D)-np.angle(B))
        factor=np.exp((0.5*blend)*(logmag + 1j*phase))
        A2=A*factor
        A2=symmetrize_fft(A2)
        an=fft.ifft(A2).real[:n]
        an=np.maximum(an,0)
        S=an.sum()
        if S < 1e-12: break
        an=an/S
        an_sc=quick_score(an) # maybe reuse? We can compute from an; expensive but okay.
        if an_sc < best_sc: nonimp=0 else nonimp+=1
        a=an
        if nonimp > 50: 
            # random branch/perturb
            a=add_noise(a,0.02,None?) Need rng? spectral_project no rng. Could break or use deterministic random? We can pass rng.
```
Add `rng` param to spectral_project. If no improvement, perturb and reset nonimp. Use `add_noise(a,0.02,rng)`.
This recomputes D each iter; heavy but okay. Maybe limit iterations by deadline. We can set `deadline` slice.

Optimization: If `lam` target close to c, factor near1; one can skip when D≈B. Not needed.

`multiplicative_topk(a0, K, eta, deadline, rng)`:
Inside, top selection each iter. We need direct b. Use `derivative_from_weights( a,p, work_limit) /2`.
For p dense? topK K maybe <=64; direct. For K large, derivative FFT; we can pass K<=64.
```
def multiplicative_topk(a0,K,eta,deadline,rng):
   a=normalize(a0); best...; n; fail=0
   while time<deadline:
      c=linear_convolve(a); cmax=c.max(); score=2n*cmax
      if score<best: update; fail=0 else fail++ (maybe only after iteration)
      if K<=1: idx argmax; p...
      else idx argpartition top min(K,n?) 
      p=zeros; p[idx]=... maybe weighted topK with beta 50? Use weighted to focus.
      Lmean=...
      h=derivative_from_weights(a,p, 3000000)
      b=0.5*h
      raw = -eta * (n*n) * a * (b - Lmean)
      cmean=float(np.dot(a,raw)) ; raw -= cmean
      raw=np.clip(raw,-0.4,0.4)
      an=a*np.exp(raw); S=an.sum(); if S>0 an/=S
      new_score=quick_score(an)
      if new_score < score - 1e-12:
          a=an; fail=0; eta=min(eta*1.05, 0.1)
      else:
          fail+=1
          eta*=0.75
          if fail>100 or eta<1e-4: break
   return best
```
Need compute score again after update; we already have score from a, new_score. But in next loop will recompute. OK.
Maybe use exact top mean for step not max. If K=1, Lmean=cmax, b derivative of max. Good.
Weighted topK: choose topK, beta=50. For K=1, p=1.

Propose detailed flow (no inner functions):
```
def propose_candidate seed=42 budget_s=2400:
    global _STOP_DEADLINE
    t0=time.monotonic(); total=float(budget_s)
    reserve=150.0
    deadline=t0+total-reserve

```
`normalize_sequence`: if a is scalar? Use `np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()`. If empty, return np.ones(64)/64? But sequence length? For empty, return np.ones(64)/64 to avoid. If `sum` not finite, clip.
```
def normalize_sequence(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
       return np.ones(64)/64.0
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
       return np.ones(a.size)/float(a.size)
    a=a/S
    return a
```

`next_len`:
```
def next_len(n):
    n=int(max(1,n))
    try:
       return int(fft.next_fast_len(n))
    except Exception:
       m=1
       while m<n: m <<=1
       return m
```

`linear_convolve`: ensure a 1d.
```
def linear_convolve(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    L=2*n-1
    M=next_len(L)
    A=fft.rfft(a,M)
    out=fft.irfft(A*A,M)[:L]
    out=np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    out[out < 0.0] = 0.0
    return out
```

`quick_score`:
```
def quick_score(a):
    a=normalize_sequence(a)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    c=linear_convolve(a)
    if c.size==0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*np.max(c))
```
Since normalized S=1. If a sum normalized but maybe values >1? Each ≤1 if sum1.

`resample_sequence`: If target n =0? not.
```
def resample_sequence(a,n):
    a=normalize_sequence(a)
    n=int(n)
    if n<=0: n=64
    m=a.size
    if m==n: return a.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    return normalize_sequence(out)
```

```
def make_shape(n, shape_id, rng=None):
    n=int(n)
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    if shape_id == 0: a=np.ones(n)
    ...
    elif shape_id == 8:
       a=np.ones(n)
       if rng is not None:
          for r in range(4):
             f=int(rng.integers(1,7))
             ph=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi))
             amp=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.5))
             a = a * np.maximum(0.0, 1.0 + amp*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*f*x + ph))
    else: a=np.ones(n)
    return normalize_sequence(a)
```

`derivative_from_weights` with p possibly length >L? We can assume L. For safety trim/pad.
```
def derivative_from_weights(a,p,work_limit=3000000):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); n=a.size
    L=2*n-1
    p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
    if p.size < L:
       tmp=np.zeros(L); tmp[:p.size]=p; p=tmp
    else:
       p=p[:L]
    pmax=float(p.max()) if p.size else 0.0
    tol=1e-15*pmax + 1e-300
    nz=np.where(p > tol)[0]
    h=np.zeros(n,float)
    if nz.size ==0: return h
    if int(nz.size) * n <= int(work_limit):
       for jj in nz:
          m=int(jj); w=float(p[jj])
          if w <= 0: continue
          i0=m-(n-1)
          if i0<0: i0=0
          i1=m
          if i1>n-1: i1=n-1
          if i1>=i0:
             low=m-i1
             high=m-i0
             # low>=0, high<n
             h[i0:i1+1] += 2.0*w*a[low:high+1][::-1]
       return h
    p_rev=p[::-1]
    M2=next_len(3*n-2)
    A=fft.fft(a,M2)
    P=fft.fft(p_rev,M2)
    conv=fft.ifft(A*P).real[:n+L]
    d=conv[n-1:L][::-1].copy()
    return 2.0*d
```
Potential issue: `a[low:high+1][::-1]` creates copy; for many peaks overhead okay. For direct work limit 3e6, total copied ≤3e6 per eval.
```

def top_indices(c,K):
   Lc=c.size; K=int(max(1,min(K,Lc)))
   if K<=1: return np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
   if K>=Lc: return np.arange(Lc)
   return np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
```

Detailed:
```
def spectral_project(a0, target_mode, blend, deadline, rng=None):
    a=normalize_sequence(a0)
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1; M=next_len(L)
    base=make_target(L,target_mode)
    best=a; best_sc=quick_score(a)
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(12345)
    it=0; nonimp=0; blend_cur=blend
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it +=1
       A=fft.fft(a,M)
       B=A*A
       c=fft.ifft(B).real[:L]
       c=np.nan_to_num(c,0,0,0); c[c<0]=0
       sc=2*n*c.max()
       if sc<best_sc: best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
       # if it every 50, reduce blend? 
       blend_cur = blend * max(0.4, 1.0 - 0.001*it)
       progress = min(1.0, it/100.0)
       lam = 0.2 + 0.65*progress
       target=(1.0-lam)*c + lam*base
       S=float(target.sum())
       if S>1e-12: target=target/S
       else: target=base
       ext=np.empty(M,float); ext[:L]=target; ext[L:]=0.0
       D=fft.fft(ext)
       Babs=np.abs(B); Dabs=np.abs(D)
       tol=1e-300
       logmag=np.log(Dabs+tol)-np.log(Babs+tol)
       logmag=np.clip(logmag,-6.0,6.0)
       phase=0.5*(np.angle(D)-np.angle(B))
       factor=np.exp(0.5*blend_cur*(logmag+1j*phase))
       A2=A*factor
       A2=symmetrize_fft(A2)
       an=fft.ifft(A2).real[:n]
       an=np.nan_to_num(an,0,0,0)
       an=np.maximum(an,0.0)
       S=float(an.sum())
       if S < 1e-12:
          an=best.copy()
       else:
          an/=S
       an_sc=quick_score(an)
       if an_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
          best=an.copy(); best_sc=an_sc; nonimp=0
       else:
          nonimp +=1
       a=an
       if nonimp > 60:
          a=add_noise(a,0.02,rng)
          nonimp=0
    return best,best_sc
```
Potential problem: `quick_score(an)` computes FFT another time. We already have c for a not an. OK. For speed, we could compute score from `an` using linear_convolve; needed. It doubles FFT (A/B and quick). We can maybe after an compute c_an and score inside, but same cost. OK.
If n large, recompute D each iter heavy. Could update target only every 10 and reuse D. But okay for limited iterations. Maybe reduce iterations by deadline slice (e.g. 120s). Each iter 2 FFT + D FFT =3 FFT 65536 ~0.06s, 2000 iter 120s. Fine.

`multiplicative_topk` code. Use top_indices and weighted p maybe. For K pass. Use `beta_frac`? We can use weighted topK with beta=50 for smooth. Implement:
```
def weighted_topk_weights(c, K, beta_frac):
   idx=top_indices(c,K)
   if idx.size <=1: return idx, np.ones_like(idx)/idx.size
   cidx=c[idx]
   cmaxidx=cidx.max()
   if beta_frac <=0 or cmaxidx<=0:
      return idx, np.full(idx.size,1/idx.size)
   scaled=beta_frac*(cidx-cmaxidx)/cmaxidx

def topk_weights(c,K,beta_frac):
    idx=top_indices(c,K)
    if idx.size==0: ...
    if idx.size==1 or beta_frac<=0: return idx, np.array([1.0])

```
Loop:
```
def multiplicative_topk(a0,K,eta,deadline,rng=None):
   a=normalize(a0); best=a; best_sc=quick_score(a)
   n=a.size; fail=0; it=0
   if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(54321)
   K=int(max(1,K))
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       c=linear_convolve(a)
       cmax=float(c.max()); score=2*n*cmax
       if score < best_sc -1e-12: best=a.copy(); best_sc=score
       idx,w=topk_weights(c,K,50.0)
       p=np.zeros(2*n-1); p[idx]=w
       Lval=float(np.dot(c[idx],w))
       h=derivative_from_weights(a,p,3000000)
       b=0.5*h
       scale = float(n)*float(n)
       raw = -eta * scale * a * (b - Lval)
       center = float(np.dot(a,raw))
       raw = raw - center
       raw = np.clip(raw, -0.35, 0.35)
       an = a * np.exp(raw)
       S=float(an.sum())
       if S < 1e-12:
          an=a
       else: an=an/S
       new_score=quick_score(an)
       if new_score < score - 1e-12:
          a=an; fail=0; eta=min(eta*1.03, 0.15)
       else:
          fail += 1; eta *= 0.75
          if fail > 80 or eta < 1e-4:
             # perturb and maybe continue
             a=add_noise(a,0.01,rng)
             fail=0
             if it > 200: break
   return best,best_sc
```
Note we recompute score for a each loop; after perturb, loop recompute. If new accepted, `score` variable from old a but next loop uses a=an. Good.
```

```
Implement `topk_weights`:
```
def topk_weights(c, K, beta_frac):
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    Lc=c.size
    if Lc==0: return np.array([],int), np.array([],float)
    K=int(max(1,min(K,Lc)))
    if K <= 1:
        return np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int), np.array([1.0])
    if K >= Lc:
        idx=np.arange(Lc,dtype=int)
    else:
        idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:].astype(int)
    if beta_frac <= 0.0:
        return idx, np.full(idx.size, 1.0/float(idx.size))
    cidx=c[idx]
    cmaxidx=float(cidx.max())
    if cmaxidx <= 1e-300:
        return idx, np.full(idx.size, 1.0/float(idx.size))
    scaled=(beta_frac*(cidx-cmaxidx))/cmaxidx
    scaled=np.clip(scaled, -700.0, 0.0)
    e=np.exp(scaled)
    esum=float(e.sum())
    if esum <= 1e-300:
        return idx, np.full(idx.size,1/idx.size)
    w=e/esum
    return idx,w
```
`argpartition` for K maybe Lc-1? okay.

For mode0 full LSE, p tail with exp. Need p normalized; if esum small? e max 1, so esum≥1. Good.

`lbfgs_objective` if mode==2 and K maybe 0? K param could 0; topk_weights sets 1. Good.
```

```python
"""
Optimizer for minimizing 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2 over non-negative sequences.

The program combines:
- FFT-based convolution,
- corrected gradient of smooth top-peak / log-sum-exp surrogates,
- L-BFGS-B polishing with sum regularization,
- spectral convolution-target projection as a global move,
- multiplicative projected-gradient refinement,
- length resampling search.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math
import scipy.fft as fft
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_STOP_DEADLINE = 0.0


def score_key(item):
    return float(item[0])


def normalize_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def next_len(n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    try:
        return int(fft.next_fast_len(n))
    except Exception:
        m = 1
        while m < n:
            m <<= 1
        return m


def linear_convolve(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    M = next_len(L)
    A = fft.rfft(a, M)
    out = fft.irfft(A * A, M)[:L]
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    out[out < 0.0] = 0.0
    return out


def quick_score(a):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = linear_convolve(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c)))


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = int(max(8, n))
    m = a.size
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_sequence(out)


def make_shape(n, shape_id, rng=None):
    n = int(max(8, n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    if shape_id == 0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    elif shape_id == 1:
        a = 1.0 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * x)
    elif shape_id == 2:
        a = 1.0 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    elif shape_id == 3:
        a = 1.0 + 0.5 * np.sin(np.pi * x)
    elif shape_id == 4:
        a = np.exp(-4.0 * (x - 0.5) * (x - 0.5))
    elif shape_id == 5:
        a = np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 2
    elif shape_id == 6:
        a = np.cos(0.5 * np.pi * x) ** 2
    elif shape_id == 7:
        a = 1.0 + 0.4 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x) + 0.2 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    elif shape_id == 8:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        if rng is not None:
            for _ in range(4):
                f = int(rng.integers(1, 7))
                ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.50))
                a = a * np.maximum(0.0, 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph))
    else:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    return normalize_sequence(a)


def make_target(L, mode):
    L = int(max(1, L))
    k = np.arange(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if mode == 0:
        t = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
    elif mode == 1:
        t = 1.0 + 0.40 * np.cos(np.pi * k / max(1.0, float(L - 1)))
    elif mode == 2:
        t = 1.0 + 0.40 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k / float(L))
    elif mode == 3:
        t = 1.0 + 0.25 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k / float(L))
    else:
        t = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.maximum(t, 1e-12)
    t /= float(t.sum())
    return t


def top_indices(c, K):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=int)
    K = int(max(1, min(int(K), L)))
    if K <= 1:
        return np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
    if K >= L:
        return np.arange(L, dtype=int)
    return np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:].astype(int)


def derivative_from_weights(a, p, work_limit=3000000):
    """
    Returns h_i = 2 * sum_m p_m a_{m-i}.
    This is the derivative of sum_m p_m c_m with respect to a_i.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size < L:
        tmp = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        tmp[:p.size] = p
        p = tmp
    else:
        p = p[:L]

    pmax = float(p.max()) if p.size else 0.0
    tol = 1e-15 * pmax + 1e-300
    nz = np.where(p > tol)[0]
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if nz.size == 0:
        return h

    if int(nz.size) * n <= int(work_limit):
        for jj in nz:
            m = int(jj)
            w = float(p[jj])
            if w <= 0.0:
                continue
            i0 = m - (n - 1)
            if i0 < 0:
                i0 = 0
            i1 = m
            if i1 > n - 1:
                i1 = n - 1
            if i1 >= i0:
                low = m - i1
                high = m - i0
                h[i0:i1 + 1] += 2.0 * w * a[low:high + 1][::-1]
        return h

    p_rev = p[::-1]
    conv_len = 3 * n - 2
    M2 = next_len(conv_len)
    A = fft.fft(a, M2)
    P = fft.fft(p_rev, M2)
    conv = fft.ifft(A * P).real[:conv_len]
    d = conv[n - 1:L][::-1].copy()
    return 2.0 * d


def lbfgs_objective(a, mode, beta_n, K, lambda_reg, work_limit):
    """
    Objective and gradient for L-BFGS-B.

    mode=0: centered log-sum-exp over all convolution lags.
    mode=1: mean of top-K convolution lags.
    mode=2: centered log-sum-exp over top-K convolution lags.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-10:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c = linear_convolve(a)
    Lc = c.size
    if Lc == 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 1e-300:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(beta_n) * float(n)
    p = np.zeros(Lc, dtype=np.float64)

    if mode == 0:
        if beta > 1e-12:
            scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
            scaled = np.clip(scaled, -700.0, 0.0)
            e = np.exp(scaled)
            esum = float(e.sum())
            if esum <= 1e-300:
                Lval = cmax
                p[:] = 1.0 / float(Lc)
            else:
                Lval = cmax + (float(np.log(esum)) - float(np.log(Lc))) / beta
                p = e / esum
        else:
            Lval = float(c.mean())
            p[:] = 1.0 / float(Lc)

    elif mode == 2:
        idx = top_indices(c, K)
        cidx = c[idx]
        Klen = int(idx.size)
        if Klen == 0:
            Lval = cmax
            p[:] = 1.0 / float(Lc)
        else:
            cmaxidx = float(cidx.max())
            if beta > 1e-12 and Klen > 1:
                scaled = beta * (cidx - cmaxidx)
                scaled = np.clip(scaled, -700.0, 0.0)
                e = np.exp(scaled)
                esum = float(e.sum())
                if esum <= 1e-300:
                    Lval = float(cidx.mean())
                    p[idx] = 1.0 / float(Klen)
                else:
                    Lval = cmaxidx + (float(np.log(esum)) - float(np.log(Klen))) / beta
                    p[idx] = e / esum
            else:
                Lval = float(cidx.mean())
                p[idx] = 1.0 / float(Klen)

    else:
        idx = top_indices(c, K)
        cidx = c[idx]
        Klen = int(idx.size)
        if Klen == 0:
            Lval = cmax
            p[:] = 1.0 / float(Lc)
        else:
            Lval = float(cidx.mean())
            p[idx] = 1.0 / float(Klen)

    if not np.isfinite(Lval) or Lval < 0.0:
        Lval = cmax

    h = derivative_from_weights(a, p, work_limit)
    S2 = S * S
    S3 = S2 * S
    if S2 <= 1e-30:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    grad = (2.0 * n) * (h / S2 - (2.0 * Lval) / S3)
    reg = float(lambda_reg) * (S - 1.0)
    grad = grad + reg

    val = (2.0 * n) * Lval / S2 + 0.5 * float(lambda_reg) * (S - 1.0) * (S - 1.0)

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)
    return float(val), grad


def stop_callback(xk):
    return time.monotonic() > _STOP_DEADLINE


def add_noise(a, sigma, rng):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    sigma = float(max(0.0, sigma))
    if sigma > 0.0:
        noise = sigma * rng.standard_normal(a.size)
        a = a * (1.0 + noise)
    small = 1e-5 / max(1.0, float(np.sqrt(a.size)))
    a = a + small * rng.random(a.size)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    return normalize_sequence(a)


def run_lbfgs_schedule(a0, deadline, rng):
    global _STOP_DEADLINE

    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    if a.size == 0 or quick_score(a) not in np.isfinite:
        a = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048.0

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = quick_score(best_a)
    n = a.size

    entries = []
    if n <= 20000:
        entries.append((0, 20.0, 0.0))
    if n <= 32768:
        entries.extend([
            (2, 10.0, 0.020),
            (2, 30.0, 0.008),
            (2, 100.0, 0.003),
            (2, 300.0, 0.001),
            (1, 0.0, 0.0005),
            (1, 0.0, 0.0002),
            (1, 0.0, 0.0),
        ])
    else:
        entries.extend([
            (2, 30.0, 0.003),
            (2, 100.0, 0.001),
            (1, 0.0, 0.0002),
            (1, 0.0, 0.0),
        ])

    if not entries:
        entries = [(1, 0.0, 0.0)]

    Kcap = 64 if n > 20000 else 128
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    total_runs = len(entries)
    fail_count = 0

    for idx, entry in enumerate(entries):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline:
            break

        mode, beta_n, kfrac = entry
        K = max(1, int(round(float(kfrac) * n)))
        K = min(K, Kcap)

        rem = max(20.0, deadline - now)
        runs_left = max(1, total_runs - idx)
        run_budget = rem / float(runs_left)
        if idx >= total_runs - 3:
            run_budget *= 1.25
        run_budget = float(min(360.0, max(25.0, run_budget)))
        run_deadline = min(float(deadline), now + run_budget)
        _STOP_DEADLINE = run_deadline

        if fail_count > 0:
            sigma = 0.002 + 0.005 * float(min(5, fail_count))
            start_a = add_noise(best_a, sigma, rng)
        else:
            start_a = best_a.copy()

        est = max(1.0, run_budget / 0.05)
        maxfun = int(min(25000, max(2000, est)))
        maxiter = int(min(20000, max(500, 2 * maxfun)))

        if n <= 16384:
            lambda_reg = 1e-3
            work_limit = 3000000
        else:
            lambda_reg = 5e-4
            work_limit = 2500000

        options = {
            "maxiter": max(1, maxiter),
            "maxfun": max(1, maxfun),
            "maxcor": 20,
            "ftol": 1e-15,
            "gtol": 1e-10,
            "maxls": 30
        }

        improved = False
        try:
            res = minimize(
                lbfgs_objective,
                start_a,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                args=(mode, float(beta_n), K, lambda_reg, work_limit),
                bounds=bounds,
                options=options,
                callback=stop_callback
            )
            if res is not None and res.x is not None:
                a_try = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                a_try = np.clip(a_try, 0.0, 1.0)
                s_try = float(a_try.sum())
                if s_try > 1e-8:
                    a_try = a_try / s_try
                    sc_try = quick_score(a_try)
                    if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_sc - 1e-12:
                        best_sc = float(sc_try)
                        best_a = a_try.copy()
                        improved = True
                        print("    L-BFGS mode={} K={} new best={:.8f}".format(mode, K, best_sc))
        except Exception as exc:
            print("    L-BFGS exception: {}".format(exc))

        if improved:
            fail_count = 0
        else:
            fail_count += 1

    return best_a, float(best_sc)


def symmetrize_fft(A):
    M = A.size
    A = np.asarray(A, dtype=np.complex128).copy()
    if M == 0:
        return A
    A[0] = A[0].real
    if M % 2 == 0:
        half = M // 2
        A[half] = A[half].real
        idx = np.arange(1, half)
    else:
        half = M // 2
        idx = np.arange(1, half + 1)

    if idx.size > 0:
        A[idx] = 0.5 * (A[idx] + np.conj(A[M - idx]))
        A[M - idx] = np.conj(A[idx])
    return A


def spectral_project(a0, target_mode, blend, deadline, rng):
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, np.inf

    L = 2 * n - 1
    M = next_len(L)
    base = make_target(L, target_mode)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = quick_score(a)
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(987654)

    it = 0
    nonimp = 0
    blank = np.zeros(M - L, dtype=np.float64) if M > L else np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1

        A = fft.fft(a, M)
        B = A * A
        c = fft.ifft(B).real[:L]
        c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        c[c < 0.0] = 0.0

        sc = 2.0 * n * float(c.max())
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a.copy()

        progress = min(1.0, float(it) / 100.0)
        lam = 0.20 + 0.65 * progress
        target = (1.0 - lam) * c + lam * base
        s_target = float(target.sum())
        if s_target <= 1e-12:
            target = base.copy()
        else:
            target = target / s_target

        ext = np.empty(M, dtype=np.float64)
        ext[:L] = target
        ext[L:] = 0.0
        D = fft.fft(ext)

        Babs = np.abs(B)
        Dabs = np.abs(D)
        logmag = np.log(Dabs + 1e-300) - np.log(Babs + 1e-300)
        logmag = np.clip(logmag, -6.0, 6.0)
        phase = 0.5 * (np.angle(D) - np.angle(B))

        cur_blend = float(max(0.15, blend * max(0.45, 1.0 - 0.001 * it)))
        factor = np.exp(0.5 * cur_blend * (logmag + 1j * phase))
        A2 = A * factor
        A2 = symmetrize_fft(A2)

        an = fft.ifft(A2).real[:n]
        an = np.nan_to_num(an, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        an = np.maximum(an, 0.0)
        s_an = float(an.sum())
        if s_an <= 1e-12:
            an = best_a.copy()
            nonimp += 1
        else:
            an = an / s_an
            an_sc = quick_score(an)
            if np.isfinite(an_sc) and an_sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(an_sc)
                best_a = an.copy()
                nonimp = 0
            else:
                nonimp += 1
        a = an

        if nonimp > 55:
            a = add_noise(a, 0.02, rng)
            nonimp = 0

    return best_a, float(best_sc)


def multiplicative_topk(a0, K, eta, deadline, rng):
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, np.inf

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = quick_score(a)
    eta = float(max(1e-6, eta))
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(24680)

    K = int(max(1, K))
    beta = 100.0 * float(n)
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = linear_convolve(a)
        cmax = float(c.max())
        score = 2.0 * n * cmax
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(score)
            best_a = a.copy()

        idx = top_indices(c, K)
        if idx.size == 0:
            break
        cidx = c[idx]
        p = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        if idx.size == 1:
            p[idx[0]] = 1.0
        else:
            cmaxidx = float(cidx.max())
            if cmaxidx <= 1e-300:
                p[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
            else:
                scaled = beta * (cidx - cmaxidx)
                scaled = np.clip(scaled, -700.0, 0.0)
                e = np.exp(scaled)
                esum = float(e.sum())
                if esum <= 1e-300:
                    p[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
                else:
                    p[idx] = e / esum

        h = derivative_from_weights(a, p, 3000000)
        b = 0.5 * h
        l_center = float(np.dot(a, b))
        raw = -eta * float(n) * float(n) * a * (b - l_center)
        raw -= float(np.dot(a, raw))
        raw = np.clip(raw, -0.35, 0.35)

        an = a * np.exp(raw)
        s_an = float(an.sum())
        if s_an <= 1e-12:
            an = a
        else:
            an = an / s_an

        new_sc = quick_score(an)
        if np.isfinite(new_sc) and new_sc < score - 1e-12:
            a = an
            if new_sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(new_sc)
                best_a = an.copy()
                stall = 0
            else:
                stall = 0
            eta = min(eta * 1.03, 0.15)
        else:
            stall += 1
            eta *= 0.75
            if stall > 90 or eta < 1e-4:
                a = add_noise(a, 0.012, rng)
                stall = 0
                if eta < 1e-4:
                    break

    return best_a, float(best_sc)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    global _STOP_DEADLINE

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(float(budget_s), 300.0)
    reserve = min(150.0, 0.20 * total)
    deadline = t0 + total - reserve

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        hv = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if hv is not None:
            p = np.asarray(hv, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 8 and float(p.sum()) > 1e-8:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        best_a = normalize_sequence(parent)
        best_sc = quick_score(best_a)
        print("Parent length={:,} internal score={:.8f}".format(best_a.size, best_sc))
    else:
        best_a = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048.0
        best_sc = quick_score(best_a)
        print("No parent found. Uniform start length=2048 score={:.8f}".format(best_sc))

    candidates = [(float(best_sc), best_a.copy())]

    lengths = [1024, 2048, 4096, 8192, 16384, 32768]
    if parent is not None:
        if parent.size <= 32768:
            lengths.append(int(parent.size))
        else:
            lengths.append(32768)
    lengths = sorted(set([int(x) for x in lengths if 8 <= int(x) <= 32768]))

    gen_end = t0 + min(120.0, max(30.0, 0.05 * (total - reserve)))
    shape_ids = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8]

    for n in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > gen_end:
            break
        if parent is not None:
            a = resample_sequence(parent, n)
            sc = quick_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                candidates.append((float(sc), a))
        for sid in shape_ids:
            if time.monotonic() > gen_end:
                break
            try:
                a = make_shape(n, sid, rng)
                sc = quick_score(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    candidates.append((float(sc), a))
            except Exception:
                pass
        if len(candidates) > 60:
            candidates.sort(key=score_key)
            candidates = candidates[:60]

    if candidates:
        candidates.sort(key=score_key)
        for sc, a in candidates[:20]:
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_a = a.copy()
        print("Initial best after generation: {:.8f} length={}".format(best_sc, best_a.size))
        if len(candidates) >= 1:
            print("Initial candidate 1: {:.8f} length={}".format(candidates[0][0], len(candidates[0][1])))
        if len(candidates) >= 2:
            print("Initial candidate 2: {:.8f} length={}".format(candidates[1][0], len(candidates[1][1])))
        if len(candidates) >= 3:
            print("Initial candidate 3: {:.8f} length={}".format(candidates[2][0], len(candidates[2][1])))

    now = time.monotonic()
    R = max(60.0, deadline - now)
    lbfgs_end = now + 0.55 * R
    spectral_end = lbfgs_end + 0.20 * R
    mult_end = spectral_end + 0.15 * R
    final_end = deadline

    if best_a.size > 0:
        print("\n=== Phase 1: L-BFGB schedule (until {:.0f}s) ===".format(lbfgs_end - t0))
        a_out, sc_out = run_lbfgs_schedule(best_a, lbfgs_end, rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc_out)
            best_a = a_out.copy()
            print("  L-BFGS schedule improved to {:.8f}".format(best_sc))

        now = time.monotonic()
        if now < lbfgs_end:
            start2 = add_noise(best_a, 0.02, rng)
            distinct_end = min(lbfgs_end, now + 0.45 * (lbfgs_end - now))
            a_out, sc_out = run_lbfgs_schedule(start2, distinct_end, rng)
            if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc_out)
                best_a = a_out.copy()
                print("  Distinct L-BFGS start improved to {:.8f}".format(best_sc))

    if best_a.size > 0:
        print("\n=== Phase 2: spectral convolution targeting (until {:.0f}s) ===".format(spectral_end - t0))
        for target_mode in [0, 1, 2]:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= spectral_end:
                break
            rem = max(20.0, spectral_end - now)
            alloc = max(20.0, min(240.0, rem / 2.0))
            try:
                a_out, sc_out = spectral_project(best_a, target_mode, 0.40, now + alloc, rng)
                if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(sc_out)
                    best_a = a_out.copy()
                    print("  Spectral target {} improved to {:.8f}".format(target_mode, best_sc))
            except Exception as exc:
                print("  Spectral exception mode={}: {}".format(target_mode, exc))

    if best_a.size > 0:
        print("\n=== Phase 3: multiplicative top-peak descent (until {:.0f}s) ===".format(mult_end - t0))
        K_list = [30, 10, 5, 3, 2, 1]
        for K in K_list:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= mult_end:
                break
            rem = max(20.0, mult_end - now)
            alloc = max(20.0, min(240.0, rem / max(1.0, float(len(K_list)))))
            try:
                a_out, sc_out = multiplicative_topk(best_a, K, 0.045, now + alloc, rng)
                if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(sc_out)
                    best_a = a_out.copy()
                    print("  Multiplicative K={} improved to {:.8f}".format(K, best_sc))
            except Exception as exc:
                print("  Multiplicative exception K={}: {}".format(K, exc))

    print("\n=== Final stage: length resampling / polish (until {:.0f}s) ===".format(final_end - t0))
    for nres in [4096, 8192, 16384, 32768]:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= final_end - 45.0 or best_a.size == 0:
            break
        if nres == best_a.size:
            continue
        try:
            a_res = resample_sequence(best_a, nres)
            sc_res = quick_score(a_res)
            if not np.isfinite(sc_res):
                continue
            if sc_res < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc_res)
                best_a = a_res.copy()
                print("  Resample to {:,} improved to {:.8f}".format(nres, best_sc))
            elif sc_res < best_sc + 0.008:
                rem = max(20.0, final_end - 45.0 - now)
                alloc = max(20.0, min(180.0, rem / 2.0))
                a_res, sc_res = run_lbfgs_schedule(a_res, now + alloc, rng)
                if np.isfinite(sc_res) and sc_res < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(sc_res)
                    best_a = a_res.copy()
                    print("  Resample+L-BFGS to {:,} improved to {:.8f}".format(nres, best_sc))

                if time.monotonic() < final_end - 45.0:
                    a_res, sc_res = multiplicative_topk(best_a, 2, 0.04, min(final_end - 45.0, time.monotonic() + 60.0), rng)
                    if np.isfinite(sc_res) and sc_res < best_sc:
                        best_sc = float(sc_res)
                        best_a = a_res.copy()
                        print("  Resample+mult to {:,} improved to {:.8f}".format(best_a.size, best_sc))
        except Exception as exc:
            print("  Final resample exception n={}: {}".format(nres, exc))

    now = time.monotonic()
    if now < final_end - 20.0 and best_a.size > 0:
        a_out, sc_out = run_lbfgs_schedule(best_a, final_end - 20.0, rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc_out)
            best_a = a_out.copy()

    final_a = normalize_sequence(best_a)
    final_sc = quick_score(final_a)

    # Prefer a shorter length if its score is essentially the same, to keep final evaluation fast.
    if final_a.size > 20000:
        short_n = 16384 if final_a.size > 16384 else 8192
        try:
            a_short = resample_sequence(final_a, short_n)
            sc_short = quick_score(a_short)
            if np.isfinite(sc_short) and sc_short <= final_sc + 2e-4:
                final_a = a_short
                final_sc = float(sc_short)
        except Exception:
            pass

    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in final_a]
    if sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [0.5] * 64

    verified = None
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = None

    if verified is None or not np.isfinite(verified) or verified > final_sc + 1e-8:
        if parent is not None:
            fallback = normalize_sequence(parent)
        else:
            fallback = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048.0
        out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in fallback]
        if sum(out) < 0.01:
            out = [0.5] * 64
        try:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        except Exception:
            verified = None

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    print("\nFinal internal score: {:.10f}".format(final_sc))
    if verified is not None:
        print("Final verified score: {:.10f}".format(verified))
    print("Final length: {:,}".format(len(out)))
    print("Elapsed: {:.1f}s / {:.1f}s".format(elapsed, total))

    return out
```
